- **memhub-staging**: STAGING build of the MemHub plugin — pointed at api.staging.memhub.xtrace.ai. For XTrace developers only; installs from your local clone, so it reflects whatever branch you have checked out (use `staging`). A path source is required here, not git-subdir: this plugin's skills/hooks/scripts are symlinks into ../memhub/, and a git-subdir fetch pulls only its own subdirectory, leaving them dangling. Registers an MCP server named `memhub`, so it collides with the public `memhub` plugin: install one or the other, never both. ([XTraceAI/agent-plugins](https://github.com/XTraceAI/agent-plugins/tree/HEAD/memhub-staging) · ★2)
